1613:  const c=carBy(carId),points=routePoints(d),start=stamp(d.date,d.time);let km=0,hours=0,days=1,billedKm=0,cab=0,allowance=0;

1616:  else if(d.service==='airport'){billedKm=km;cab=Math.round(Math.max(1200,km*c.one+300)*c.multiplier);}

1688: function paymentPanel(b,short=false){

1693: function statusLabel(b){return b.status==='arrived'?'Driver arrived':b.status==='onway'?'Driver on the way':b.status==='cancelled'?'Cancelled':b.status==='completed'?'Completed':b.status==='ongoing'?'Ongoing':b.request?.type==='cancel'?'Cancel request pending':b.driver?'Driver assigned':'Confirmed';}
1694: function tripCard(b){const ds=driverStatus(b);return`<button type="button" class="nc-tripcard cursor-interaction" data-action="open" data-id="${esc(b.id)}"><div class="nc-tripcard-inner"><div class="nc-row">${pill(serviceName(b.draft.service))}${pill(statusLabel(b),b.status==='cancelled'?'red':b.status==='completed'?'':'green')}</div><div class="nc-triproute">${routeTitle(b.draft)}</div><div class="nc-row"><span class="nc-small nc-muted">${fullDate(b.pickupAt)}</span><strong class="nc-small">${money(finalTotal(b))}</strong></div></div><div class="nc-strip">${ico(b.driver?'badge-check':b.status==='completed'?'circle-check':'clock-3')}<span>${esc(b.status==='completed'?t('Trip completed'):b.status==='cancelled'?b.cancelReason:ds.label)}</span>${ico('chevron-right')}</div></button>`;}

1696: function trips(){const list=state.bookings.filter(b=>tab==='Upcoming'?['confirmed','assigned','onway','arrived'].includes(b.status):tab==='Ongoing'?b.status==='ongoing':tab==='Completed'?b.status==='completed':b.status==='cancelled');return`${head('My Bookings','Every journey, in one place.','account')}<div class="nc-tabs">${['Upcoming','Ongoing','Completed','Cancelled'].map(x=>B('tab',x,'',tab===x?'active':'',`data-tab="${x}" aria-pressed="${tab===x}"`)).join('')}</div><div class="nc-list">${list.length?list.map(tripCard).join(''):empty()}</div>`;}
1697: function progress(b){const labels=['Booking confirmed','Driver assigned','Driver on the way','Driver arrived','Trip started','Trip completed'];let n=b.status==='completed'?5:b.status==='ongoing'?4:b.status==='arrived'?3:b.status==='onway'?2:b.driver?1:0;return`<div class="nc-panel"><h3>${esc(t('Trip progress'))}</h3><ol class="nc-timeline">${labels.map((label,k)=>{const ev=b.events.find(e=>e.label===label);return`<li><span class="nc-timemark ${k<=n?'done':''}">${ico(k<=n?'check':'minus')}</span><div><strong>${esc(t(label))}</strong><p>${ev?fullDate(ev.at):k===1&&!b.driver?esc(driverStatus(b).label):'—'}</p></div></li>`;}).join('')}</ol></div>`;}
1698: function booking(){const b=current();if(!b)return empty();const d=b.draft;return`${head('Booking details',b.id,'trips')}<div class="nc-row">${pill(serviceName(d.service))}${pill(statusLabel(b),b.status==='cancelled'?'red':'green')}</div><div class="nc-triproute">${routeTitle(d)}</div><p class="nc-small nc-muted">${fullDate(b.pickupAt)} · ${esc(carBy(b.car).name)}</p><div class="nc-panel nc-gap-lg"><h3>Journey details</h3>${routePoints(d).map((a,n)=>`<div class="nc-segment">${ico(n===0?'circle-dot':'map-pin')}<span class="nc-address">${esc(a)}</span></div>`).join('')}${d.service==='round'?`<div class="nc-segment">${ico('repeat-2')}Return · ${fullDate(stamp(d.returnDate,d.returnTime))}</div>`:''}${d.flight?`<div class="nc-segment">${ico('plane')}Flight · ${esc(d.flight)}</div>`:''}<div class="nc-divider"></div><p class="nc-small"><strong>${esc(t('Passenger'))}</strong> · ${esc(b.passenger)}</p><p class="nc-small nc-muted">+91 ${esc(b.mobile)}</p>${d.notes?`<p class="nc-small nc-muted nc-gap">${esc(d.notes)}</p>`:''}</div>${b.status==='cancelled'?notice(esc(b.cancelReason),'red','circle-x'):driverPanel(b)}${['arrived','ongoing'].includes(b.status)?`<div class="nc-panel nc-center"><h3>${esc(t('Boarding OTP'))}</h3>${b.status==='arrived'?`<div class="nc-otp">${b.otp.split('').map(n=>`<span>${n}</span>`).join('')}</div><p class="nc-small nc-muted">Share only when you are with your assigned driver and ready to board.</p>`:notice('OTP verified. Your trip has started.','','shield-check')}</div>`:''}${b.status==='completed'&&b.final?`<div class="nc-panel"><h3>${esc(t('Trip completed'))}</h3><div class="nc-farerow"><span>Actual distance</span><strong>${b.final.actualKm} km</strong></div>${b.fare.hours?`<div class="nc-farerow"><span>Actual duration</span><strong>${b.final.actualHours} hrs</strong></div>`:''}<p class="nc-small nc-muted nc-gap">Toll, parking and state tax are settled separately against receipts.</p></div>`:''}${b.status!=='cancelled'?farePanel(b,b.status==='completed'):''}${paymentPanel(b)}${b.status!=='cancelled'&&b.status!=='completed'?progress(b):''}${b.status==='completed'?`<div class="nc-panel nc-gap-lg nc-center"><h3>${esc(t('Rate your trip'))}</h3><div class="nc-stars">${[1,2,3,4,5].map(n=>B('star',String(n)+' star','star',n<=(rating||b.rating)?'selected':'',`data-rating="${n}" aria-label="${n} star" aria-pressed="${n<=(rating||b.rating)}"`)).join('')}</div>${b.rating?`<p class="nc-small nc-muted">Thank you for your feedback.</p>`:B('rating','Submit rating','','nc-secondary')}</div>`:''}<div class="nc-panel nc-gap-lg"><h3>${esc(t('Need help with this trip?'))}</h3><a class="nc-listrow" href="tel:+919025099990">${ico('phone')}<span class="nc-grow">${esc(t('Call NESAM'))}</span>${ico('chevron-right')}</a>${['confirmed','assigned','onway','arrived'].includes(b.status)?`${B('request-change','Request a change','calendar-clock','nc-listrow')}${cancellationTools(b)}`:''}${b.request?notice('Your '+(b.request.type==='cancel'?'cancellation':'change')+' request is with the office. The booking remains active until the office confirms.','amber'):''}</div>`;}
1699: function payment(){
1700:  const b=current();if(!b)return empty();
1701:  if(!canStartPayment(b)||balance(b)===0)return`${head('Payment',b.id,'booking')}<div class="nc-triproute">${routeTitle(b.draft)}</div>${paymentPanel(b)}`;

1703:  return`${head('Payment',b.id,'booking')}<div class="nc-panel"><div class="nc-row"><h3>${routeTitle(b.draft)}</h3>${pill(paymentView(b).label,paymentView(b).kind)}</div><div class="nc-farerow"><span>${esc(t(b.status==='cancelled'?'Cancellation charge':'Total fare'))}</span><strong>${money(b.status==='cancelled'?(b.cancellationCharge||0):finalTotal(b))}</strong></div><div class="nc-farerow"><span>${esc(t('Paid'))}</span><strong>${money(b.paid)}</strong></div><div class="nc-farerow nc-total"><span>${esc(t(b.status==='completed'?'Final amount payable':'Balance'))}</span><strong>${money(due)}</strong></div></div>${hasPendingPayment(b)?notice(esc(t('Payment is being verified. Do not pay again.')),'amber'): `<div class="nc-panel"><h3>${esc(t('Choose how much to pay'))}</h3>${advance?`<button type="button" class="nc-payoption" data-action="pay-choice" data-choice="advance" aria-pressed="${paymentChoice==='advance'}">${ico('wallet')}<span style="flex:1"><strong>${esc(t('Pay advance'))}</strong><small>${esc(t('25% of booking fare'))}</small></span><strong>${money(advance)}</strong></button>`:''}<button type="button" class="nc-payoption" data-action="pay-choice" data-choice="full" aria-pressed="${paymentChoice==='full'}">${ico('credit-card')}<span style="flex:1"><strong>${esc(t(b.status==='completed'?'Pay balance':'Pay full amount'))}</strong><small>${esc(t('Clear the outstanding balance'))}</small></span><strong>${money(due)}</strong></button></div>${B('checkout',b.status==='completed'?'Pay balance':'Pay now','lock-keyhole')}`}<p class="nc-small nc-muted nc-center nc-gap">${esc(t('UX demo · No real payment is collected.'))}</p>`;
1704: }

1726: function cancellationQuote(b,at=state.now,noShow=false){const p=policyFor(b);let charge=0,reason='Free cancellation';if(noShow){const wait=(b.draft.service==='airport'?p.airportWaitMinutes:p.waitMinutes)*60000;const proof=b.arrivalProof;if(!b.driver||b.status!=='arrived'||!proof?.correctPickup||!proof?.officeVerified||at<Math.max(b.pickupAt,proof.at)+wait)throw Error('No-show needs verified arrival, office review and the full pickup grace period.');charge=p.noShowCharge;reason='Office-verified no-show';}else if(at<b.pickupAt&&b.pickupAt-at>=p.freeHours*3600000){reason='Within free cancellation window';}else if(!b.driver){reason='Driver not assigned';}else{charge=p.assignedCharge;reason='Driver assigned · within '+p.freeHours+' hours of pickup';}charge=Math.min(charge,b.fare.total);return{policyId:p.id,at,charge,reason,paid:b.paid,refund:Math.max(0,round2(b.paid-charge)),amountDue:Math.max(0,round2(charge-b.paid)),noShow};}
1727: function approveCancellation(b,waive=false){if(!b?.request||b.request.type!=='cancel')throw Error('Submit a cancellation request first.');if(!['confirmed','assigned','onway','arrived'].includes(b.status))throw Error('This booking cannot be cancelled from this request.');if(b.payments.some(x=>x.status==='pending'))throw Error('Resolve the pending payment before processing cancellation.');const q=b.request.quote;if(!q||!b.request.chargeConsent)throw Error('The customer must review and accept the cancellation quote first.');const charge=waive?0:q.charge;b.cancellationCharge=charge;b.cancelReason=waive?'Customer cancellation · office waived charge':'Customer cancellation · office approved';b.cancelledAt=state.now;b.acceptedCancellationQuote=clone(q);b.status='cancelled';b.refund={amount:Math.max(0,round2(b.paid-charge)),status:b.paid>charge?'initiated':'not-needed',at:state.now,method:'Original payment method'};b.request=null;alertUser('Cancellation approved. Charge '+money(charge)+'. Refund '+money(b.refund.amount)+'.',b.id);}

2093: function routeSearchKey(r){return [r.service,...[r.pickup,r.drop].map(s=>s.trim().replace(/\s+/g,' ').toLowerCase())].join('|');}
2094: function uniqueRecentRoutes(list){const seen=new Set();return (Array.isArray(list)?list:[]).filter(r=>{if(!r||!['oneway','round'].includes(r.service)||typeof r.pickup!=='string'||typeof r.drop!=='string')return false;const key=routeSearchKey(r);if(seen.has(key))return false;seen.add(key);return true;}).slice(0,2);}

2110: function hasPendingPayment(b){return !!b?.payments?.some(p=>p.status==='pending');}
2111: function canStartPayment(b){return !!b&&(['confirmed','assigned','onway','arrived','cancelled'].includes(b.status)||(b.status==='completed'&&!!b.final));}
2112: function paymentView(b){if(b.status==='ongoing')return{label:'Final bill after trip',kind:'neutral'};if(b.status==='completed'&&!b.final)return{label:'Final bill pending',kind:'neutral'};if(b.status==='cancelled')return{label:b.refund?.amount?(b.refund.status==='completed'?'Refund completed':'Refund initiated'):'Cancelled',kind:'neutral'};if(hasPendingPayment(b))return{label:'Payment processing',kind:'amber'};if(balance(b)===0)return{label:'Paid ✓',kind:'green'};return{label:b.status==='completed'?'Balance due':'Payment pending',kind:'amber'};}
2113: function tripPaymentNote(b){if(b.status==='ongoing')return`<div class="nc-final-bill-note"><strong>${esc(t('Final bill after trip'))}</strong>${esc(t('Your final fare will be calculated when the trip ends.'))}</div>`;if(b.status==='completed'&&!b.final)return`<div class="nc-final-bill-note">${esc(t('Your final bill is being prepared.'))}</div>`;if(hasPendingPayment(b))return`<div class="nc-final-bill-note">${esc(t('Payment is being verified. 2158: function activeBeforeTrip(b){return !!b&&['confirmed','assigned','onway','arrived'].includes(b.status);}
2159: policyDefault=function(){return {id:'NSM-CUSTOMER-2026-10-V2',selfCancelMinutes:60,arrivalCharge:300,noShowCharge:500,waitMinutes:30,airportWaitMinutes:60};};
2160: policyFor=function(b){return {...policyDefault(),...(b?.policy||{}),selfCancelMinutes:60,arrivalCharge:300};};
2161: function canDirectCancel(b,at=state.now){return activeBeforeTrip(b)&&at<b.pickupAt-policyFor(b).selfCancelMinutes*60000&&!hasPendingPayment(b)&&!b.request;}
2162: function verifiedArrival(b,at=state.now){const proof=b?.arrivalProof;return !!(b?.driver&&b.status==='arrived'&&proof?.correctPickup===true&&proof?.officeVerified===true&&Number.isFinite(proof.at)&&proof.at<=at&&(proof.at>=b.pickupAt||proof.customerRequestedEarly===true));}
2163: policyMarkup=function(p=policyDefault(),pickupAt){const cutoff=pickupAt?fullDate(pickupAt-60*60000):'';return `<div class="nc-policy"><div class="nc-policyrow"><span>${esc(t('More than 1 hour before pickup'))}</span><strong>${esc(t('Cancel in app'))}</strong></div><div class="nc-policyrow"><span>${esc(t('Within 1 hour of pickup'))}</span><strong>${esc(t('Call office'))}</strong></div><div class="nc-policyrow"><span>${esc(t('Office-verified driver arrival'))}</span><strong>${money(300)}</strong></div>${cutoff?`<p class="nc-small nc-muted nc-gap">${esc(t('App cancellation closes at'))} ${cutoff} IST.</p>`:''}<p class="nc-small nc-muted nc-gap">${esc(t('Before the cutoff, confirm cancellation in the app. After the cutoff, call NESAM. A driver arrival charge applies only after the office verifies arrival at the correct pickup. Early arrival requires your request.'))}</p><p class="nc-small nc-muted nc-gap">${esc(t('Any advance covers the charge first; the rest is refunded. An office-confirmed unpaid cancellation charge must be cleared before your next booking is confirmed. Cash paid to the driver is counted after office confirmation.'))}</p><p class="nc-small nc-muted nc-gap">${esc(t('Early arrival alone is not a no-show.'))} ${esc(t('Verified no-show after the pickup grace period'))}: ${money(p.noShowCharge||500)} · ${p.waitMinutes||30} min (${p.airportWaitMinutes||60} min ${esc(t('for airports'))}).</p><p class="nc-small nc-muted nc-gap">${esc(t('Office can review or waive a charge for valid concerns. If NESAM cannot provide the cab, all paid amounts are refunded.'))}</p></div>`;};
2164: cancellationQuote=function(b,at=state.now,noShow=false){if(!b)throw Error('Open a booking first.');const p=policyFor(b);let charge=0,reason='No driver arrival charge';if(noShow){const proof=b.arrivalProof,wait=(b.draft.service==='airport'?p.airportWaitMinutes:p.waitMinutes)*60000;if(!b.driver||b.status!=='arrived'||!proof?.correctPickup||!proof?.officeVerified||at<Math.max(b.pickupAt,proof.at)+wait)throw Error('No-show needs verified arrival and the full waiting period after pickup.');charge=p.noShowCharge;reason='Office-verified no-show';}else if(verifiedArrival(b,at)){charge=p.arrivalCharge;reason='Office-verified driver arrival cancellation';}charge=Math.min(charge,b.fare.total);return {policyId:p.id,at,charge,reason,paid:b.paid,refund:Math.max(0,round2(b.paid-charge)),amountDue:Math.max(0,round2(charge-b.paid)),noShow};};
2165: function syncCancellationRefund(b){const old=b.refund,processed=Math.max(old?.processedAmount||0,old?.status==='completed'?old.amount:0),amount=Math.max(0,round2(b.paid-(b.cancellationCharge||0))),remaining=Math.max(0,round2(amount-processed));b.refund={...(old||{}),amount,processedAmount:processed,remaining,status:amount<=0?'not-needed':remaining<=0?'completed':'initiated',at:old?.at||state.now,method:'Original payment method'};}
2166: function syncDriverSettlement(b){if(b.driverSettlement){b.driverSettlement.collected=Math.min(b.cancellationCharge||0,b.paid);b.driverSettlement.amount=b.cancellationCharge||0;b.driverSettlement.status=b.cancellationReview?.status==='under-review'?'under-review':b.cancellationCharge===0?'waived':balance(b)>0?'awaiting-payment':'recorded';}}
2167: function finalizeCancellation(b,q,{source='customer',reason='',note='',waive=false}={}){if(!activeBeforeTrip(b))throw Error('This trip cannot be cancelled.');if(hasPendingPayment(b))throw Error('Payment is being verified. Call NESAM before cancelling.');const charge=waive?0:q.charge;b.cancellationCharge=charge;b.cancelledAt=state.now;b.cancelReason=reason||q.reason;b.cancellationSource=source;b.cancellationDetails=note;b.acceptedCancellationQuote=clone(q);b.cancellationReview={status:waive?'waived':'confirmed',officeVerified:source==='office'||(charge>0&&verifiedArrival(b)),at:state.now};b.status='cancelled';b.request=null;b.events.push({label:'Booking cancelled',at:state.now});if(b.driver&&charge>0)b.driverSettlement={driverId:b.driver.id,amount:charge,collected:Math.min(charge,b.paid),status:'recorded'};syncCancellationRefund(b);syncDriverSettlement(b);b.cancellationUpdates=[{recipient:'office',status:'recorded in preview',at:state.now},...(b.driver?[{recipient:'driver',driverId:b.driver.id,status:'recorded in preview',at:state.now}]:[])];alertUser('Booking cancelled. Charge '+money(charge)+(b.refund.amount?'. Refund '+money(b.refund.amount):balance(b)>0?'. Balance '+money(balance(b)):''),b.id);}
2168: approveCancellation=function(b,waive=false){if(!b?.request||b.request.type!=='cancel')throw Error('No cancellation request is pending.');const r=b.request;if(!r.quote||!r.chargeConsent)throw Error('Review the cancellation amount first.');finalizeCancellation(b,r.quote,{source:'office',reason:r.reason,note:r.note,waive});};

2239: function playConfirmationChime(bookingId=null,preview=false){
2240:  const context=confirmationAudio;
2241:  if(!confirmationSoundEnabled()||!context||context.state!=='running'||document.hidden)return false;
2242:  if(bookingId&&confirmationSoundsPlayed.has(bookingId))return false;
2243:  try{
2244:   const start=context.currentTime+.015;
2245:   // Two rounded bell notes, with quiet upper partials and a gentle 0.7s decay.
2246:   const partials=[{ratio:1,level:.078,release:.49},{ratio:2.005,level:.014,release:.36},{ratio:3.01,level:.004,release:.28}];
2247:   [783.99,1046.5].forEach((frequency,n)=>{
2248:    const at=start+n*.19;
2249:    partials.forEach(({ratio,level,release})=>{
2250:     const tone=context.createOscillator(),gain=context.createGain();
2251:     tone.type='sine';
2252:     tone.frequency.setValueAtTime(frequency*ratio,at);
2253:     gain.gain.setValueAtTime(.0001,at);
2254:     gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(level,at+.01);
2255:     gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(level*.42,at+.1);
2256:     gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.001,at+release-.05);
2257:     gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,at+release);
2258:     tone.connect(gain);gain.connect(context.destination);
2259:     confirmationVoices.add(tone);
2260:     tone.onended=()=>{confirmationVoices.delete(tone);tone.disconnect();gain.disconnect();};
2261:     tone.start(at);tone.stop(at+release+.02);
2262:    });
2263:   });
2264:   if(bookingId){confirmationSoundsPlayed.add(bookingId);confirmationStats.chimes++;}
2265:   if(preview)confirmationStats.previews++;
2266:   return true;
2267:  }catch{return false;}

2447:   if (desktopTitle) desktopTitle.textContent = mobileTitle;
2448:   booking.querySelector('.nc-service-context')?.remove();
2449:   const swap = booking.querySelector('[data-action="swap"]');
2450:   if (swap) {
2451:    swap.className = 'nc-swap-control';
2452:    swap.setAttribute('aria-label', t('Swap pickup and drop'));
2453:    swap.innerHTML = ico('arrow-up-down');
2454:    const fields = booking.querySelector('.nc-fields');
2455:    if (fields) {
2456:     const group = document.createElement('div');
2457:     group.className = 'nc-route-fields';
2458:     fields.before(group);
2459:     group.appendChild(fields);
2460:     group.appendChild(swap);
2461:    }
2462:    booking.querySelector('.nc-route-tools')?.remove();
2463:   }
2464:  }
2465:  return holder.innerHTML;
2466: };

2478: const nsHandleBefore = handleV2;
2479: handleV2 = async function(action, data = {}) {
2480:  if (action === 'app-download') { nsAppDownloadOpen = !nsAppDownloadOpen; return true; }
2481:  if (action === 'app-download-close') { nsAppDownloadOpen = false; return true; }
2482:  nsAppDownloadOpen = false;
2483:  if (action === 'popular-toggle') { nsRoutesExpanded = !nsRoutesExpanded; return true; }
2484:  if (action === 'outstation-open') {
2485:   state.outstationTab = nsLastOutstation;
2486:   return await nsHandleBefore('service', {service:nsLastOutstation});
2487:  }
2488:  if (action === 'route-shortcut' && data.kind === 'popular') {
2489:   if (!nsOutstationTabs.includes(state.draft.service)) await nsHandleBefore('service', {service:nsLastOutstation});
2490:   const result = await nsHandleBefore(action, data);
2491:   nsLastOutstation = state.draft.service;
2492:   state.outstationTab = nsLastOutstation;
2493:   return result;
2494:  }
2495:  const result = await nsHandleBefore(action, data);
2496:  if (action === 'service' && nsOutstationTabs.includes(data.service)) {
2497:   nsLastOutstation = data.service;
2498:   state.outstationTab = nsLastOutstation;
2499:  }
2500:  if (action === 'reset-confirm') { nsLastOutstation = 'oneway'; state.outstationTab = 'oneway'; nsRoutesExpanded = false; }
2501:  return result;
2502: };

2678: // Customer progress has the five agreed milestones. Older stored "onway"
2679: // records remain at Driver Assigned until a real arrival update is recorded.
2680: const nsCustomerMilestones = [
2681:  ['Booking Confirmed','Booking confirmed'],
2682:  ['Driver Assigned','Driver assigned'],
2683:  ['Driver Arrived','Driver arrived'],
2684:  ['Trip Started','Trip started'],
2685:  ['Trip Completed','Trip completed']
2686: ];
2687: for (const milestone of nsCustomerMilestones) TA[milestone[0]] = TA[milestone[1]] || milestone[0];
2688: progress = function(b) {
2689:  const currentStep = b.status==='completed' ? 4 : b.status==='ongoing' ? 3 : b.status==='arrived' ? 2 : b.driver ? 1 : 0;
2690:  return '<div class="nc-panel"><h3>' + esc(t('Trip progress')) + '</h3><ol class="nc-timeline">' + nsCustomerMilestones.map(function(step,index) {
2691:   const event = b.events.find(function(item) { return item.label.toLowerCase()===step[1].toLowerCase(); });
2692:   const when = event ? fullDate(event.at) : index===1 && !b.driver ? esc(driverStatus(b).label) : '—';
2693:   return '<li><span class="nc-timemark ' + (index<=currentStep?'done':'') + '">' + ico(index<=currentStep?'check':'minus') + '</span><div><strong>' + esc(t(step[0])) + '</strong><p>' + when + '</p></div></li>';
2694:  }).join('') + '</ol></div>';
2695: };
